# Awesome-Selfhosted with stars

[![Awesome](_static/awesome.png)](https://github.com/sindresorhus/awesome) ⭐ 501,241 | 🐛 105 | 📅 2026-08-21 [![](https://github.com/awesome-selfhosted/awesome-selfhosted-data/actions/workflows/check-dead-links.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/awesome-selfhosted/awesome-selfhosted-data/issues/1) ⭐ 1,121 | 🐛 202 | 🌐 Makefile | 📅 2026-08-30 [![](https://github.com/awesome-selfhosted/awesome-selfhosted-data/actions/workflows/check-unmaintained-projects.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/awesome-selfhosted/awesome-selfhosted-data/issues/1) ⭐ 1,121 | 🐛 202 | 🌐 Makefile | 📅 2026-08-30 [![](https://img.shields.io/liberapay/goal/awesome-selfhosted.svg?logo=liberapay)](https://liberapay.com/awesome-selfhosted/)

Self-hosting is the practice of hosting and managing applications on your own server(s) instead of consuming from 🌎 [SaaSS](www.gnu.org/philosophy/who-does-that-server-really-serve.html) providers.

This is a list of 🌎 [Free](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_software) Software 🌎 [network services](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Network_service) and 🌎 [web applications](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_application) which can be hosted on your own server(s). Non-Free software is listed on the [Non-Free](https://github.com/correia-jpv/fucking-awesome-selfhosted/blob/master/non-free.md) ⭐ 148 | 🐛 1 | 📅 2026-08-28 page.

* 🌎 [HTML version](awesome-selfhosted.net/) (recommended)\*\*, [Markdown version](https://github.com/correia-jpv/fucking-awesome-selfhosted) ⭐ 148 | 🐛 1 | 📅 2026-08-28 (legacy).

See [Contributing](#contributing).

An 🌎 [integrated library system](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Integrated_library_system) is an enterprise resource planning system for a library, used to track items owned, orders made, bills paid, and patrons who have borrowed.

*Related: [Content Management Systems (CMS)](#content-management-systems-cms), [Archiving and Digital Preservation (DP)](#archiving-and-digital-preservation-dp)*

* 🌎 [Koha](koha-community.org/) - Enterprise-class ILS with modules for acquisitions, circulation, cataloging, label printing, offline circulation for when Internet access is not available, and much more.  🌎 [Demo](koha-community.org/demo/), <b><code>   586⭐</code></b> <b><code>   300🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/Koha-Community/Koha) ⭐ 586 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Perl | 📅 2026-08-28)) `GPL-3.0` `Perl`
* 🌎 [Evergreen](evergreen-ils.org) - Highly-scalable software for libraries that helps library patrons find library materials, and helps libraries manage, catalog, and circulate those materials. (<b><code>   158⭐</code></b> <b><code>   113🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/evergreen-library-system/Evergreen) ⭐ 158 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 PLpgSQL | 📅 2026-08-27)) `GPL-2.0` `PLpgSQL`
* 🌎 [RERO ILS](rero21.ch/) - Large-scale ILS that can be run as a service with consortial features, intended primarily for library networks. Includes most standard modules (circulation, acquisitions, cataloging,...) and a web-based public and professional interface.  🌎 [Demo](ils.test.rero.ch/), <b><code>    87⭐</code></b> <b><code>    30🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/rero/rero-ils) ⭐ 87 | 🐛 48 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-29)) `AGPL-3.0` `Python/Docker`

### Groupware

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**

Collaborative software or 🌎 [groupware](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Collaborative_software) is designed to help people working on a common task to attain their goals. Groupware often regroups multiple services such as file sharing, calendar/events management, appointment scheduling, address books... in a single, integrated application.

### Media Streaming

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**
🌎 [Streaming media](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Streaming_media) is multimedia that is delivered and consumed in a continuous manner from a source, with little or no intermediate storage in network elements.

**Please visit [Media streaming - Audio Streaming](#media-streaming---audio-streaming), [Media streaming - Multimedia Streaming](#media-streaming---multimedia-streaming), [Media streaming - Video Streaming](#media-streaming---video-streaming), [Media Management](#media-management)**

### Proxy

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**

A 🌎 [proxy](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proxy_server) is a server application that acts as an intermediary between a client requesting a resource and the server providing that resource. This section about forward (i.e. outgoing) proxies. For reverse proxies, see the Web Server section.

*Related: [Web Servers](#web-servers)*

* 🌎 [imgproxy](imgproxy.net/) - Fast and secure standalone server for resizing and converting remote images. (<b><code> 11034⭐</code></b> <b><code>   771🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/imgproxy/imgproxy) ⭐ 11,035 | 🐛 70 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-27)) `MIT` `Go/Docker/K8S`
* 🌎 [iodine](code.kryo.se/iodine/) - IPv4 over DNS tunnel solution, enabling you to start up a socks5 proxy listener. (<b><code>  7957⭐</code></b> <b><code>   596🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/yarrick/iodine) ⭐ 7,958 | 🐛 29 | 🌐 C | 📅 2025-09-04)) `ISC` `C/deb`
* 🌎 [Outline Server](getoutline.org/) - A proxy server that runs a Shadowsocks instance for each access key and a REST API to manage the access keys. (<b><code>  6250⭐</code></b> <b><code>   878🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/OutlineFoundation/outline-server) ⭐ 6,250 | 🐛 185 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-05-13)) `Apache-2.0` `Docker/Nodejs`
* 🌎 [Tinyproxy](tinyproxy.github.io/) - Light-weight HTTP/HTTPS proxy daemon. (<b><code>  5976⭐</code></b> <b><code>   759🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/tinyproxy/tinyproxy) ⭐ 5,976 | 🐛 140 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-07-29)) `GPL-2.0` `C/deb`
* <b><code>  4705⭐</code></b> <b><code>   333🍴</code></b> [sish](https://github.com/antoniomika/sish) ⭐ 4,706 | 🐛 23 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-06-25) - HTTP(S)/WS(S)/TCP tunnels to localhost using only SSH (serveo/ngrok alternative). `MIT` `Go/Docker`
* 🌎 [g3proxy](g3-project.readthedocs.io/projects/g3proxy/en/latest/) - Forward proxy server supporting proxy chaining, protocol inspection, MITM Interception, ICAP adaptation and transparent proxy. (<b><code>   895⭐</code></b> <b><code>    81🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/bytedance/g3/tree/master/g3proxy) ⭐ 895 | 🐛 25 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-08-27)) `Apache-2.0` `Rust/deb`
* 🌎 [GitProxy](git-proxy.finos.org/) - Proxy for Git that applies rules and workflows to all outgoing git push operations and ensures they are compliant. It supports both HTTP/HTTPS and SSH protocols with security scanning and validation. (<b><code>   242⭐</code></b> <b><code>   174🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/finos/git-proxy) ⭐ 243 | 🐛 97 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-08-29)) `Apache-2.0` `Nodejs/Docker`
* <b><code>   143⭐</code></b> <b><code>    26🍴</code></b> [socks5-proxy-server](https://github.com/nskondratev/socks5-proxy-server) ⭐ 143 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-03-21) - SOCKS5 proxy server with built-in authentication and Telegram-bot for user management and user statistics on data spent (handy when you pay per GB of data). It is dockerised and simple to install. `Apache-2.0` `Docker`

### URL Shorteners

🌎 [URL shortening](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/URL_shortening) is the action of shortening a 🌎 [URL](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uniform_Resource_Locator) to make it substantially shorter and still direct to the required page. Before hosting one, please see 🌎 [disadvantages](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/URL_shortening#Disadvantages) of URL shorteners.

### VPN

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**

A 🌎 [virtual private network (VPN)](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_private_network) extends a private network across a public network and enables users to send and receive data across shared or public networks as if their computing devices were directly connected to the private network.

**Please visit <b><code> 35010⭐</code></b> <b><code>  2104🍴</code></b> [awesome-sysadmin/VPN](https://github.com/awesome-foss/awesome-sysadmin#vpn) ⭐ 35,023 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-08-29)**

*See also: <b><code> 21673⭐</code></b> <b><code>  1060🍴</code></b> [Awesome-Tunneling](https://github.com/anderspitman/awesome-tunneling) ⭐ 21,679 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-11)*

### Web Servers

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**

Web Servers and Reverse Proxies. A 🌎 [web server](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_server) is a piece of software and underlying hardware that accepts requests via 🌎 [HTTP](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hypertext_Transfer_Protocol) (the network protocol created to distribute web content) or its secure variant 🌎 [HTTPS](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTTPS). A 🌎 [Reverse Proxy](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reverse_proxy) is a proxy server that appears to any client to be an ordinary web server, but in reality merely acts as an intermediary that forwards requests to one or more ordinary web servers.

*Related: [Proxy](#proxy)*

* 🌎 [Caddy](caddyserver.com/) - Powerful, enterprise-ready, open source web server with automatic HTTPS. (<b><code> 75276⭐</code></b> <b><code>  4918🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/caddyserver/caddy) ⭐ 75,332 | 🐛 269 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-28)) `Apache-2.0` `Go/deb/Docker`
* 🌎 [Traefik](traefik.io/) - HTTP reverse proxy and load balancer that makes deploying microservices easy. (<b><code> 64626⭐</code></b> <b><code>  6167🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/traefik/traefik) ⭐ 64,660 | 🐛 915 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-28)) `MIT` `Go/Docker`
* 🌎 [Nginx Proxy Manager](nginxproxymanager.com/) - Docker container for managing Nginx proxy hosts with a simple, powerful interface. (<b><code> 34009⭐</code></b> <b><code>  3870🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/NginxProxyManager/nginx-proxy-manager) ⭐ 34,019 | 🐛 897 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-08-29)) `MIT` `Docker`
* 🌎 [NGINX](nginx.org/en/) - HTTP and reverse proxy server, mail proxy server, and generic TCP/UDP proxy server. (<b><code> 31517⭐</code></b> <b><code>  8281🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/nginx/nginx) ⭐ 31,529 | 🐛 476 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-08-26)) `BSD-2-Clause` `C/deb/Docker`
* 🌎 [Pangolin](digpangolin.com/) - Identity-aware tunneled reverse proxy with dashboard UI, access control, and WireGuard-based tunnels (alternative to Cloudflare Tunnel, Tailscale). (<b><code> 22515⭐</code></b> <b><code>   768🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/fosrl/pangolin) ⭐ 22,549 | 🐛 122 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-08-28)) `AGPL-3.0` `Docker`
* 🌎 [SafeLine](waf.chaitin.com/) - Web application firewall / reverse proxy to protect your web apps from attacks and exploits.  🌎 [Demo](demo.waf.chaitin.com/), <b><code> 22458⭐</code></b> <b><code>  1513🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/chaitin/SafeLine) ⭐ 22,465 | 🐛 64 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-26)) `GPL-3.0` `Docker`
* 🌎 [BunkerWeb](www.bunkerweb.io) - Next-gen Web Application Firewall (WAF) that will protect your web services.  🌎 [Demo](demo.bunkerweb.io), <b><code> 10876⭐</code></b> <b><code>   641🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/bunkerity/bunkerweb) ⭐ 10,881 | 🐛 157 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-28), 🌎 [Clients](docs.bunkerweb.io/latest/plugins/)) `AGPL-3.0` `deb/Docker/K8S/Python`
* 🌎 [Zoraxy](zoraxy.aroz.org/) - General purpose HTTP reverse proxy and forwarding tool. (<b><code>  5416⭐</code></b> <b><code>   320🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/tobychui/zoraxy) ⭐ 5,415 | 🐛 161 | 🌐 HTML | 📅 2026-08-23)) `AGPL-3.0` `Go/Docker`
* 🌎 [Pomerium](www.pomerium.io) - Identity-aware reverse proxy, successor to now obsolete oauth\_proxy. It inserts an OAuth step before proxying your request to the backend, so that you can safely expose your self-hosted websites to public Internet. (<b><code>  4979⭐</code></b> <b><code>   350🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/pomerium/pomerium) ⭐ 4,982 | 🐛 154 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-28)) `Apache-2.0` `Go/Docker`
* <b><code>  4116⭐</code></b> <b><code>   188🍴</code></b> [go-doxy](https://github.com/yusing/godoxy) ⭐ 4,119 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-08-28) - Lightweight, simple, and  performant reverse proxy with WebUI, Docker integration, automatic shutdown/startup for container based on traffic. `MIT` `Docker/Go`
* <b><code>  3714⭐</code></b> <b><code>   276🍴</code></b> [SWAG (Secure Web Application Gateway)](https://github.com/linuxserver/docker-swag) ⭐ 3,716 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 Dockerfile | 📅 2026-08-29) - Nginx webserver and reverse proxy with PHP support, built-in Certbot (Let's Encrypt) client and fail2ban integration. `GPL-3.0` `Docker`

(<b><code>  3027⭐</code></b> <b><code>   151🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/xyproto/algernon) ⭐ 3,027 | 🐛 19 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-08-27)) `BSD-3-Clause` `Go/Docker`
* 🌎 [Static Web Server](static-web-server.net/) - Cross-platform, high-performance, and asynchronous web server for static file serving. (<b><code>  2340⭐</code></b> <b><code>   131🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/static-web-server/static-web-server) ⭐ 2,341 | 🐛 36 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-08-20)) `Apache-2.0/MIT` `Rust/Docker`
* 🌎 [Ferron](ferron.sh/) - Fast, memory-safe web server written in Rust. (<b><code>  2123⭐</code></b> <b><code>    94🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/ferronweb/ferron) ⭐ 2,123 | 🐛 71 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-08-30)) `MIT` `Rust/Docker/deb`
* 🌎 [UUSEC WAF](waf.uusec.com/) - Industry-leading high-performance, AI and semantic technology web application firewall and API security gateway (fork of nginx). (<b><code>  1704⭐</code></b> <b><code>   170🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/Safe3/uusec-waf) ⭐ 1,706 | 🐛 83 | 🌐 Shell | 📅 2026-08-23)) `GPL-3.0` `C/Lua/Docker`

* 🌎 [HAProxy](www.haproxy.org/) - Very fast and reliable reverse-proxy offering high availability, load balancing, and proxying for TCP and HTTP-based applications.  🌎 [Source Code](git.haproxy.org/?p=haproxy.git;a=tree)) `GPL-2.0` `C/deb/Docker`

### Wikis

**[`^        back to top        ^`](#awesome-selfhosted)**

A 🌎 [wiki](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wiki) is a publication collaboratively edited and managed by its own audience directly using a web browser.

*Related: [Note-taking & Editors](#note-taking--editors), [Static Site Generators](#static-site-generators), [Knowledge Management Tools](#knowledge-management-tools)*

*See also: 🌎 [Wikimatrix](www.wikimatrix.org/), 🌎 [List of wiki software - Wikipedia](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_wiki_software), 🌎 [Comparison of wiki software - Wikipedia](en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_wiki_software)*

* 🌎 [Wiki.js](js.wiki/) - Modern, lightweight and powerful wiki app using Git and Markdown.  🌎 [Demo](docs.requarks.io), <b><code> 28813⭐</code></b> <b><code>  3297🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/Requarks/wiki) ⭐ 28,818 | 🐛 189 | 🌐 Vue | 📅 2026-08-30)) `AGPL-3.0` `Nodejs/Docker/K8S`
* 🌎 [docmost Community Edition](docmost.com/) - Collaborative wiki and documentation software (alternative to Confluence, Notion). (<b><code> 21500⭐</code></b> <b><code>  1538🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/docmost/docmost) ⭐ 21,513 | 🐛 317 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-08-29)) `AGPL-3.0` `Docker/Nodejs`
* <b><code> 14318⭐</code></b> <b><code>  1560🍴</code></b> [Gollum](https://github.com/gollum/gollum) ⭐ 14,320 | 🐛 91 | 🌐 Ruby | 📅 2025-11-24) - Simple, Git-powered wiki with a sweet API and local frontend. `MIT` `Ruby`
* 🌎 [TiddlyWiki](tiddlywiki.com/) - Reusable non-linear personal web notebook. (<b><code>  8632⭐</code></b> <b><code>  1250🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/TiddlyWiki/TiddlyWiki5) ⭐ 8,632 | 🐛 1,161 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-08-29)) `BSD-3-Clause` `Nodejs`
* 🌎 [Dokuwiki](www.dokuwiki.org/DokuWiki) - Easy to use, lightweight, standards-compliant wiki engine with a simple syntax allowing reading the data outside the wiki. All data is stored in plain text files, therefore no database is required. (<b><code>  4708⭐</code></b> <b><code>   920🍴</code></b> [Source Code](https://github.com/dokuwiki/dokuwiki) ⭐ 4,708 | 🐛 466 | 🌐 PHP | 📅 2026-08-27)) `GPL-2.0` `PHP`
